{"amberApp":1,"files":{"/README.md":"# Lift\n\nA strength-training logger: routines, a live workout with rest timer, an exercise library, history, records and body weight.\n\n## Screens\n- Workout (src/screens/home.tsx): start empty or from a routine; routines in folders, reorder, edit\n- Live workout (src/screens/workout.tsx, route /workout): sets, previous values, rest timer, supersets, finish summary\n- History (src/screens/history.tsx): calendar, workout list and detail\n- Exercises (src/screens/exercises.tsx): ~100 exercises + custom, search and filters, per-exercise charts/records/history\n- Progress (src/screens/progress.tsx): week's muscle distribution, volume charts, recent PRs, body weight\n- Settings (src/screens/settings.tsx): units, rest defaults, RPE, plate calculator, demo data\n\n## Data (data.json, values; weights are always kg)\n- workouts: Workout[] oldest first: { id, name, start, end, notes?, exs: [{ id, ex, ss?, rest, note?, sets: [{ w, r, rpe?, t: N|W|D|F, done }] }] }\n- routines: Routine[] { id, name, folder?, exs: same shape as workout exs }\n- folders: { id, name }[]\n- active: the running workout or null (also holds restEnd / restTotal for the rest timer)\n- customExercises: { id, name, muscle, equip, custom }[] (the built-in library is in src/lib/library.ts)\n- body: { id, date: YYYY-MM-DD, kg, bf? }[]\n- settings: { unit: kg|lb, rest, autoRest, rpe, barKg, barLb, offKg[], offLb[] }\n- seeded: true once the 9-week demo data was written on first open\n\n## How this app runs in Amber Notes\nVite + React + TypeScript + Tailwind + shadcn/ui, compiled by Amber Notes on save.
